Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the 3d framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 820. The resulting operator achieves bound O(87/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 820 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the 3d structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 87. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the 3d space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 758 and the convergence rate is O(85/n^2).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the 3d space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 758 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 85. QED.
